Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARGETAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPS_ICIN_TRANSFER","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "STOK25_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"
	'Dim Par
	'Set Par=Nothing
	'Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK2512_M",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if trim(STACK_PDA.PROPT) = "1" Then
		Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
		if MT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu Barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
			Exit Sub
		End if

		if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if D7LB.SKOD <> MTableT.KOD(1) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan Etiketin Stok Kodu öncekinden farklý olamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
				MTable.BARKOD = ""
				STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
				Exit Sub
			End if
		End if
		Call StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Else
		Call Cikar()
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()	
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		YeniAlloc = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO & _
							STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

		AskiAlloc = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(1) & _
							MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(1)

		if Trim(YeniAlloc) <> trim(EskiAlloc) Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Rezervasyon bilgisi farklý olan etiketler birleþtirilemez", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub	
		End if
	End if


	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE", _
					"ALLOC_SYSSTATUS", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", "ALLOC_SIPTYPE", "ALLOC_SIPNO", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", _
					"ALLOC_MPSEVNO", "ALLOC_MPSARTNO", "STOK03_ID")

	For each Alan in S01Arr
		AlanDegeri = STOK01_PDA.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
		MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
	Next
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Cikar()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)

	Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
	Else
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if MTableT.SERINO(i) = D7LB.SERINO Then
				MTableT.KOD(i) = ""
				MTableT.SF_UNIT(i) = ""
				MTableT.SafePack()
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

'	YeniL1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
'	YeniL2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
'	YeniL3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
'	YeniL4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = YeniL1
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = YeniL2
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = YeniL3
'	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = YeniL4

	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", "KOD='" & D7LB.SKOD & "'&SERINO='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan barkoda ait stok kaydý bulunamadý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if S01.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan barkoda ait birden çok stok satýrý bulundu", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	STOK01_PDA.KOD= D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD

	MTable.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE

	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	STOK01_PDA.STOK03_ID = S01.STOK03_ID

	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = S01.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 1) = "S" Then
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(MTable.BARKOD,2), 30)
	Else
		Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	if trim(MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Kod/Lot/Seri Transfer Fiþi iptal edildi", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
	End if
End Sub
Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()

	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S25E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)

	S25T.Empty

	Set S01List = MTableT
	For i = 1 to S01List.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S25T.AddRow()
		S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		S25T.KOD = S01List.KOD(i)
		S25T.LOTNUMBER = S01List.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S25T.SERINO = S01List.SERINO(i)
		S25T.SF_MIKTAR = S01List.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT = S01List.SF_UNIT(i)
		S25T.FOKOD = S01List.FOKOD(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C = S01List.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01List.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01List.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01List.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1 = S01List.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2 = S01List.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION1 = S01List.LOCATION1(i)
		S25T.LOCATION2 = S01List.LOCATION2(i)
		S25T.LOCATION3 = S01List.LOCATION3(i)
		S25T.LOCATION4 = S01List.LOCATION4(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01List.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01List.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01List.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01List.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01List.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01List.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01List.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01List.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01List.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		S25T.AMBCODE_TR = S01List.AMBCODE(i)
	Next

	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"

	Set S25T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdfg34gfg5t5", "STOK25T", "", "<SF_MIKTAR", 1)
	YeniSeriNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.SetCurrentRow(i)
		S25T.NEWCODE = S25T1.KOD(1)
		S25T.NEWSERINO = YeniSeriNo
		S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER = S25T1.LOTNUMBER(1)
		S25T.NEWFOKOD = S25T1.FOKOD(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_C(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_R1(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_R2(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_VR_R3(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_NUM1(1)
		S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2 = S25T1.OSF_VRI_NUM2(1)
		S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S25T1.AMBCODE_TR(1)
		S25T.NEWLOCATION1 = ""
		S25T.NEWLOCATION2 = ""
		S25T.NEWLOCATION3 = ""
		S25T.NEWLOCATION4 = ""
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then 
		MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		Call YeniEtiketKartlariniYarat(Doc1, S25T)
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
	MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO = TREvrakNo
End Sub
Sub YeniEtiketKartlariniYarat(Doc2, S25T1)
	Set S25T = Doc2.Select2G("sdf34rg5ggr", S25T1.GetAppName, "", "", "NEWSERINO", -1)
	Set S25T2 = Doc2.Select2("sdf34rg5gg__r", S25T1.GetAppName, "", "<SF_MIKTAR", 1)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(i)), 30), "")) = "" Then
			if not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
				DocYaratildi = True
				Set D7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
			End if
			if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T1.SERINO(i)), 30), "")) <> "" Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T1.SERINO(i)
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
				D7LB.SERINO = S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				D7LB.ONCEKETIKET = S25T2.SERINO(1)
				D7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				D7LB.AMBCODE1 = S25T2.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
			Else
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
				d7LB.SKOD = S25T.NEWCODE(i)
				d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK25"
				d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = S25T1.EVRAKNO
				d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i)
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_UNIT = S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
				d7LB.FOKOD = S25T.NEWFOKOD(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_C = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
				d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)		
				D7LB.AMBCODE1 = S25T2.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
			End if
			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_ETKBR")
	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if trim(TREvrakNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK25E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO, "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	if S25T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdgf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(1)),30) & "'", "", -1)
		if D7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			EtiketFormKodu = D7LB.FORMKODU
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu, "PrintLabel"
		End if
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub ExceleGonder()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.EXCELOUT", 	"SendTableToExcel", MTableT
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BPRS13 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG6
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Atölyeden operasyon tamamlanma bildirimi
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set Doc3 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "BOZUK","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_NO","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SON_OPMU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_SAYISI","DOUBLE",3,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "YARIMAMULMU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "IMALATAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""


	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SONRAKI_OP","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKTYPE","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"

End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if trim(MTable.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Barkod = UCase(Barkod)
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "*", "-")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, ".", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "Ç", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "ç", "%")

	Hata = False
	if Left(Barkod, 4) = "%JB%" Then
		IsNo = Mid(Barkod, 5)
	Else
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdg34hshrs", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if trim(d7LB.JOBNO) <> "" Then
				IsNo = GetNextJobNo(d7LB.JOBNO)
				if trim(IsNo) = "" Then
					Hata = True
				End if
			Else
				Hata = True
			End if
		Else
			Hata = True
		End if
		if Hata Then
 			Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("asdfwrhsfh", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(IsNo), 16) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox IsNo & " no.lu iþ geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	Call StokBilgileriniDoldur(M10T)

	MTable.BARKOD = ""
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()	
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Call CalcOpMiktar(MTable.MPSNO, MTable.JOBNO, BuOpToplamMiktar, OncekiOpMiktar)
	'msgbox BuOpToplamMiktar & ", " & OncekiOpMiktar
	if BuOpToplamMiktar > OncekiOpMiktar and OncekiOpMiktar <> -1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Önceki Operasyonun Tamamlanan Miktarý  :" & OncekiOpMiktar & vbcrlf & "Bu operasyonun Tamamlanan miktarý : " & BuOpToplamMiktar & vbcrlf & "giriþ yapýlamaz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE", _
					"ALLOC_SYSSTATUS", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", "ALLOC_SIPTYPE", "ALLOC_SIPNO", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", _
					"ALLOC_MPSEVNO", "ALLOC_MPSARTNO", "STOK03_ID")

	For each Alan in S01Arr
		AlanDegeri = STOK01_PDA.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
		MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
	Next
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI

	MTableT.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
	MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
	MTableT.OPERASYON = MTable.OPERASYON
	MTableT.TEZGAH = MTable.TEZGAH
	MTableT.JOBNO = MTable.JOBNO

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Cikar()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
	Else
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if MTableT.SERINO(i) = D7LB.SERINO Then
				MTableT.KOD(i) = ""
				MTableT.SF_UNIT(i) = ""
				MTableT.SafePack()
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur(M10T)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.Empty

	Set M10TI = Doc.Select1("sdg3hdfh35h", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO(1) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "SRNUM", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10TI.GetRowCount()
		if M10TI.JOBNO(i) = M10T.JOBNO(1) Then
			OpSiraNo = i
			if i < M10TI.GetRowCount() Then
				MTable.SONRAKI_OP = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(i+1)
			End if
			Exit For
		End if
	Next
	STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO = OpSiraNo
	if i = M10TI.GetRowCount() Then STOK01_PDA.SON_OPMU = "E"
	STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI = M10TI.GetRowCount()

	STOK01_PDA.YARIMAMULMU = YariMamulMu(M10T.R_MAMULKODU)

	STOK01_PDA.KOD= M10T.R_MAMULKODU
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
	LotNo = trim(M10T.EVRAKNO)
	if trim(STOK01_PDA.SON_OPMU) <> "E" Then
		LotNo = LotNo & "-" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO & "/" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_SF_UNIT", M10T.EVRAKNO, "")
	if STOK01_PDA.SON_OPMU = "E" Then
		if STOK01_PDA.YARIMAMULMU = "E" Then
			Depo = "IMD"
		Else
			Depo = "MMLD"
		End if
	Else
		Depo = "YMD"
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = Depo

	if STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO = 1 Then
		STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE = "IMD"
	Else
		STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE = "YMD"
	End if


	MTable.MPSNO = M10T.EVRAKNO
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("sdf22h4dfh", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(1)
	End if
	MTable.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO
	MTable.OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON
	MTable.OPERASYON_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10T.R_OPERASYON, "")
	MTable.TEZGAH = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU
	MTable.TEZGAH_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU , "")
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	SonEvrakTuru = MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE
	if trim(SonEvrakTuru) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE) = "KD" Then
		Set S25E = Doc.Select1("sdg34hd", "STOK25E", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.SON_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S25E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
		EvrakTuru = "Stok Kod/Seri No Transfer Fiþi"
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Elseif trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE) = "MG" Then
		Set S20E = Doc.Select1("sdg34hd", "STOK20E", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.SON_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S20E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
		EvrakTuru = "Üretim Fiþi"
	End if
	ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu " & EvrakTuru & vbcrlf & "silinecek, onaylýyor musunuz?", "Dikkat", MB_YESNO)
	if ret <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.SON_EVRAKNO
	if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu " & EvrakTuru & " silindi", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
		STOK01_PDA.Empty
		Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
	End if
End Sub
Sub FisOlustur()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO = 1 Then
		ret = UretimFisiOlustur()
	Else
		ret = StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur()
	End if
	Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
	if ret = 0 Then Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-URUN", "PrintLabel"

	SonEvrakType = MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE
	SonEvrakNo = MTable.SON_EVRAKNO
	SonSeriNo = MTable.SON_SERINO

	MTable.Empty
	MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE = SonEvrakType
	MTable.SON_EVRAKNO = SonEvrakNo
	MTable.SON_SERINO = SonSeriNo

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
	Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
End Sub
Function StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur = -1

	VerenDepo = STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE

'	QStr = "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
'	QStr = QStr & "&LOTNUMBER='" & trim(MTable.MPSNO) & "-" & (STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO - 1) & "/" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI & "'"
'	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", QStr, "SERINO", -1)
	QStr = "SELECT * FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "' AND KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND LOTNUMBER LIKE '%-" & (STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO - 1) & "/" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI & "'"
	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfg23h432hdfh", QStr)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda önceki operasyondan çýkan miktar bulunamadý.", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	Elseif S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR") < (STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI + STOK01_PDA.BOZUK) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda önceki operasyondan çýkan " & S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR") & " adet var.", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE

	Call StoklariDagit(S01, S25T, STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI, "")
	Call StoklariDagit(S01, S25T, STOK01_PDA.BOZUK, "HRD")

	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i) = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
		if trim(S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(i)) <> "HRD" Then S25T.NEWSERINO(i) = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTable.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		MTable.SON_SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
'		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur = ret
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub StoklariDagit(S01, S25T, DagitilacakMiktar, Depo)
	if DagitilacakMiktar = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Kalan = DagitilacakMiktar
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if Kalan = 0 Then Exit For
		if S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			satirNo = S25T.AddRow
			S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			S25T.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
			if Kalan < S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				Miktar = Kalan
			Else
				Miktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			End if
			Kalan = Kalan - Miktar
			S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) - Miktar

			S25T.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
			S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
			S25T.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S25T.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
			S25T.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S25T.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
			S25T.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
			S25T.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
			S25T.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
			S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = Depo
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	exit sub
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if trim(TREvrakNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK25E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO, "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	if S25T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdgf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(1)),30) & "'", "", -1)
		if D7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			EtiketFormKodu = D7LB.FORMKODU
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu, "PrintLabel"
		End if
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function UretimFisiOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
'	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK20", "")
	Set STOK20E = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set STOK20T = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Doc2.New_Voucher
	STOK20E.AMBCODE = STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE
	STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE = STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE
	STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE
	
	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI > 0 Then
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
		if trim(STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM) = "" Then
			STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
		End if
		STOK20T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
		STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
		STOK20T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.BOZUK > 0 Then
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
		if trim(STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM) = "" Then
			STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
		End if
		STOK20T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
		STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
'		STOK20T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.BOZUK
		STOK20T.AMBCODE_TR = "HRD"
	End if

	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	Call HammaddeKontrol(Doc2)
	UretimFisiOlustur = Doc2.Save_Voucher

	if UretimFisiOlustur = 0 Then
		MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "MG"
		MTable.SON_EVRAKNO = STOK20E.EVRAKNO
		MTable.SON_SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Üretim Fiþi Kaydedilmedi", "Hata", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function SonOpMu(MPSNo, JobNo)
	SonOpMu = False
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", JobNo, "JOBNO")
	if trim(SrNum) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("f23f23sdgh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		SonOpMu = True
	End if
End Function
Function UretimFisiniSil(EvrakNo, TrNum)
	SeriNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SFDC32T", "SERINO", EvrakNo & TrNum, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	QStr = "W_EVRAKTYPE='S4'&W_EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&W_TRNUM='" & TrNum & "'"
	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select1("sdfwegwhs", "STOK20T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S20T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		UretimFisiniSil = 0 
		Exit Function
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher S20T1.EVRAKNO(1)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then 
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Delete_Voucher
		Exit Function
	Else
		For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S20T.SERINO(i)) = trim(SeriNo) Then
				S20T.DeleRowNoUI i
			End if
		Next
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	End if
End Function
Sub EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SFDC32T.SERINO(i)), 30)
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("adf3efsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB.SF_MIKTAR <> SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if Not DocYaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
					Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
					DocYaratildi = True
				End if
				Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
				d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub CalcOpMiktar(MPSNo, IsNo, BuOpTamamlananMiktar, OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sd2g24sdfgdsg", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM<'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = -1
	Else
		BuOpYMamulMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_YMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO")
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = Doc.Div(M10T.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR(1) * BuOpYMamulMiktar, M10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(1))
	End if
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("dfw34gsg", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = 0
	if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = MTableT.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
	BuOpTamamlananMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO") + BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar + _
						STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
End Sub
Function HammaddeYok(IsNo, UretilecekMiktar)
	HammaddeYok = False
	MPSNo = Left(IsNo, 10)
	SiraNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_SIRANO", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	MPSYMamulMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_YMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO")

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("df24gsgs", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & SiraNo & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		Depo = Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "IM")
		IhtiyacMiktari = Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(i) *  UretilecekMiktar, MPSYMamulMiktar)
		if Depo < IhtiyacMiktari Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu operasyonda kullanýlan " & trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)) & " kodlu malzeme IM deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeYok = True
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function YariMamulMu(StokKodu)
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("sdfw424dgfg", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & StokKodu & "'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		YariMamulMu = "E"
	Else
		YariMamulMu = "H"
	End if
End Function
Function GetNextJobNo(IsNo)
	GetNextJobNo = ""
	if trim(IsNo) = "" Then Exit Function

	SRNUM = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	MPSNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	if trim(MPSNo) = "" or trim(SRNUM) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdg24hdfh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SRNUM & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetNextJobNo = M10T.JOBNO(1)
End Function
Sub HammaddeKontrol(Doc2)
	Set S20TI = Doc2.Select2G("sdf34hdfhdf", "STOK20TI", "STOK01DEBULUNDUMU='2'", "", "KOD", -1)
	For i = 1 to S20TI.GetRowCount()
		Set S20TI1 = Doc2.Select2("sgvgrgdf34hdfhdf", "STOK20TI", "KOD='" & S20TI.KOD(i) & "'&STOK01DEBULUNDUMU<>'2'", "", -1)
		if S20TI1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
		if S20TI.SF_MIKTAR(i) > (S20TI1.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR") * 0.02) Then Exit Sub
	Next
	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set Doc3 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_NO","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SON_OPMU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_SAYISI","DOUBLE",3,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "YARIMAMULMU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "IMALATAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SONRAKI_OP","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKTYPE","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SON_SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TERAZI_1500","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TERAZI_100","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TERAZI_30","DOUBLE",12,6,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "BIRIM_AGIRLIK","DOUBLE",12,6,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "DARA","DOUBLE",12,6,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "NET_AGIRLIK","DOUBLE",12,6,"R","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "TEZGAH","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"

End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Barkod = UCase(Barkod)
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "*", "-")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, ".", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "Ç", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "ç", "%")

	Hata = False
	if Left(Barkod, 4) = "%JB%" Then
		IsNo = Mid(Barkod, 5)
	Else
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdg34hshrs", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if trim(d7LB.JOBNO) <> "" Then
				IsNo = GetNextJobNo(d7LB.JOBNO)
				if trim(IsNo) = "" Then
					Hata = True
				End if
			Else
				Hata = True
			End if
		Else
			Hata = True
		End if
		if Hata Then
 			Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("asdfwrhsfh", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(IsNo), 16) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox IsNo & " no.lu iþ geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Exit Sub		
	End if
'	if HammaddeYok(d7LB.JOBNO, d7LB.SF_MIKTAR) Then Exit Sub

	Call StokBilgileriniDoldur(M10T)

	MTable.BARKOD = ""
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()	
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Ekle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.KOD) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutma yapýlmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Miktar girmelisiniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Call CalcOpMiktar(MTable.MPSNO, MTable.JOBNO, BuOpToplamMiktar, OncekiOpMiktar)
	'msgbox BuOpToplamMiktar & ", " & OncekiOpMiktar
	if BuOpToplamMiktar > OncekiOpMiktar and OncekiOpMiktar <> -1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Önceki Operasyonun Tamamlanan Miktarý  :" & OncekiOpMiktar & vbcrlf & "Bu operasyonun Tamamlanan miktarý : " & BuOpToplamMiktar & vbcrlf & "giriþ yapýlamaz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE", _
					"ALLOC_SYSSTATUS", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", "ALLOC_SIPTYPE", "ALLOC_SIPNO", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", _
					"ALLOC_MPSEVNO", "ALLOC_MPSARTNO", "STOK03_ID")

	For each Alan in S01Arr
		AlanDegeri = STOK01_PDA.GetFieldValue(Alan, -1)
		MTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, -1, AlanDegeri
	Next
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI

	MTableT.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
	MTableT.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
	MTableT.OPERASYON = MTable.OPERASYON
	MTableT.TEZGAH = MTable.TEZGAH
	MTableT.JOBNO = MTable.JOBNO

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub Cikar()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "SERINO='" & D7LB.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
	Else
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if MTableT.SERINO(i) = D7LB.SERINO Then
				MTableT.KOD(i) = ""
				MTableT.SF_UNIT(i) = ""
				MTableT.SafePack()
				Exit For
			End if
		Next
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = MTableT.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur(M10T)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.Empty

	Set M10TI = Doc.Select1("sdg3hdfh35h", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO(1) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "SRNUM", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10TI.GetRowCount()
		if M10TI.JOBNO(i) = M10T.JOBNO(1) Then
			OpSiraNo = i
			if i < M10TI.GetRowCount() Then
				MTable.SONRAKI_OP = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(i+1)
			End if
			Exit For
		End if
	Next
	STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO = OpSiraNo
	if i = M10TI.GetRowCount() Then STOK01_PDA.SON_OPMU = "E"
	STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI = M10TI.GetRowCount()

	STOK01_PDA.YARIMAMULMU = YariMamulMu(M10T.R_MAMULKODU)

	STOK01_PDA.KOD= M10T.R_MAMULKODU
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
	LotNo = trim(M10T.EVRAKNO)
	if trim(STOK01_PDA.SON_OPMU) <> "E" Then
		LotNo = LotNo & "-" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO & "/" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_SF_UNIT", M10T.EVRAKNO, "")
	if STOK01_PDA.SON_OPMU = "E" Then
		if STOK01_PDA.YARIMAMULMU = "E" Then
			Depo = "IMD"
		Else
			Depo = "MMLD"
		End if
	Else
		Depo = "YMD"
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = Depo

	if STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO = 1 Then
		STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE = "IMD"
	Else
		STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE = "YMD"
	End if


	MTable.MPSNO = M10T.EVRAKNO
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("sdf22h4dfh", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(1)
	End if
	MTable.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO
	MTable.OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON
	MTable.OPERASYON_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10T.R_OPERASYON, "")
	MTable.TEZGAH = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU
	MTable.TEZGAH_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU , "")
End Sub
Sub AlanDepoveLokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub
	if uBound(LokArray) = 3 and trim(LokArray(0)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if uBound(LokArray) = 4 Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = LokArray(4)
	Else
		QStr = "LOCATION1='" & LokArray(0) & "'&LOCATION2='" & LokArray(1) & "'&LOCATION3='" & LokArray(2) & "'&LOCATION4='" & LokArray(3) & "'"
		Set S69T = Doc.Select1("f23fsdf3", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
		if S69T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = S69T.AMBCODE
		End if
	End if
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1 = LokArray(0)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2 = LokArray(1)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3 = LokArray(2)
	STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4 = LokArray(3)
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	SonEvrakTuru = MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE
	if trim(SonEvrakTuru) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE) = "KD" Then
		Set S25E = Doc.Select1("sdg34hd", "STOK25E", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.SON_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S25E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
		EvrakTuru = "Stok Kod/Seri No Transfer Fiþi"
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Elseif trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE) = "MG" Then
		Set S20E = Doc.Select1("sdg34hd", "STOK20E", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.SON_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S20E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
		EvrakTuru = "Üretim Fiþi"
	End if
	ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu " & EvrakTuru & vbcrlf & "silinecek, onaylýyor musunuz?", "Dikkat", MB_YESNO)
	if ret <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.SON_EVRAKNO
	if Doc1.Delete_Voucher = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.SON_EVRAKNO) & " no.lu " & EvrakTuru & " silindi", "Ýþlem Tamam", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
	End if
End Sub
Sub FisOlustur()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO = 1 Then
		ret = UretimFisiOlustur()
	Else
		ret = StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur()
	End if
	Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
	if ret = 0 Then Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-URUN", "PrintLabel"

	SonEvrakType = MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE
	SonEvrakNo = MTable.SON_EVRAKNO
	SonSeriNo = MTable.SON_SERINO

	MTable.Empty
	MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE = SonEvrakType
	MTable.SON_EVRAKNO = SonEvrakNo
	MTable.SON_SERINO = SonSeriNo

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
	Doc.SetFocus "BARKOD"
End Sub
Function StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur = -1

	VerenDepo = STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE

'	QStr = "AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "'&KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
'	QStr = QStr & "&LOTNUMBER='" & trim(MTable.MPSNO) & "-" & (STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO - 1) & "/" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI & "'"
'	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sd34fdfgdfg", "STOK01", QStr, "SERINO", -1)
	QStr = "SELECT STOK01.* FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN D7KIDSLB ON D7KIDSLB.SERINO=STOK01.SERINO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK01.AMBCODE='" & VerenDepo & "' AND STOK01.KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.LOTNUMBER LIKE '%-" & (STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_NO - 1) & "/" & STOK01_PDA.OPERASYON_SAYISI & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND D7KIDSLB.KOD IS NOT NULL"
	QStr = QStr & " AND D7KIDSLB.REFTEXT501 LIKE '" & MTable.JOBNO & "%'"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY D7KIDSLB.REFTEXT501"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfg23h432hdfh", QStr)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda önceki operasyondan çýkan miktar bulunamadý.", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	Elseif S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR") < (STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI + STOK01_PDA.BOZUK) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox VerenDepo & " deposunda önceki operasyondan çýkan " & S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR") & " adet var.", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = VerenDepo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE

	Call StoklariDagit(S01, S25T, STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI, "")
	Call StoklariDagit(S01, S25T, STOK01_PDA.BOZUK, "HRD")

	Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA_ALL"
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i) = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
		if trim(S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(i)) <> "HRD" Then S25T.NEWSERINO(i) = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
		MTable.SON_EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
		MTable.SON_SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
'		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	End if
	StokKodSeriNoTransferFisiOlustur = ret
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub StoklariDagit(S01, S25T, DagitilacakMiktar, Depo)
	if DagitilacakMiktar = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Kalan = DagitilacakMiktar
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if Kalan = 0 Then Exit For
		if S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			satirNo = S25T.AddRow
			S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			S25T.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
			if Kalan < S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				Miktar = Kalan
			Else
				Miktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			End if
			Kalan = Kalan - Miktar
			S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) - Miktar

			S25T.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
			S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
			S25T.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S25T.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
			S25T.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S25T.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
			S25T.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
			S25T.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
			S25T.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
			S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = Depo
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub EtiketYazdir()
	exit sub
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	TREvrakNo = MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	if trim(TREvrakNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK25E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO, "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK25_EVRAKNO
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	if S25T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdgf", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.NEWSERINO(1)),30) & "'", "", -1)
		if D7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			EtiketFormKodu = D7LB.FORMKODU
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu, "PrintLabel"
		End if
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function UretimFisiOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
'	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK20", "")
	Set STOK20E = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set STOK20T = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Doc2.New_Voucher
	STOK20E.AMBCODE = STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE
	STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE = STOK01_PDA.IMALATAMBCODE
	STOK20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE
	
	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI > 0 Then
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
		if trim(STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM) = "" Then
			STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
		End if
		STOK20T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
		STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
		STOK20T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	End if

	if STOK01_PDA.BOZUK > 0 Then
		satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
		STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK20T.MPSNO = MTable.MPSNO
		if trim(STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM) = "" Then
			STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = MTable.MPSTM_TRNUM
		End if
		STOK20T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
		STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
'		STOK20T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
		STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.BOZUK
		STOK20T.AMBCODE_TR = "HRD"
	End if

	Doc2.RunCommand "RECETE_HESAPLA_VARSA_MPS_YOKSA_STD"
'	Doc2.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	Call HammaddeLotSeriBul(Doc2)

'	Call HammaddeKontrol(Doc2)
	UretimFisiOlustur = Doc2.Save_Voucher

	if UretimFisiOlustur = 0 Then
		MTable.SON_EVRAKTYPE = "MG"
		MTable.SON_EVRAKNO = STOK20E.EVRAKNO
		MTable.SON_SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Üretim Fiþi Kaydedilmedi", "Hata", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub HammaddeLotSeriBul(Doc2)
	Set STOK20TI = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20TI")
	i = 1
	Do while i <= STOK20TI.GetRowCount()
		ret = HammaddeDagit(Doc2, i)
		i = i + 1
	Loop
End Sub
Function HammaddeDagit(Doc2, satirNo)
	HammaddeDagit = False
	Set STOK20E = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set STOK20TI = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK20TI")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if trim(STOK20TI.PM(satirNo)) = "+" Then Exit Function

	DagitilacakMiktar = STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	if DagitilacakMiktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	QStr = "SELECT STOK01.* FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN D7KIDSLB ON D7KIDSLB.SERINO=STOK01.SERINO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK01.AMBCODE='" & STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE & "' AND STOK01.KOD='" & STOK20TI.KOD(satirNo) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND D7KIDSLB.KOD IS NOT NULL"
	QStr = QStr & " AND D7KIDSLB.REFTEXT501 LIKE '" & MTable.JOBNO & "%'"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY D7KIDSLB.REFTEXT501"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfg23h432hdfh", QStr)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE) & " deposunda " & trim(STOK20TI.KOD(satirNo)) & " hammaddesi bulunamadý.", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	Elseif S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR") < STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) Then
		MsgStr = trim(STOK20E.IMALATAMBCODE)  & " deposunda " & trim(STOK20TI.KOD(satirNo)) & " hammaddesi " & _
					(STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) - S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")) & " " & trim(STOK20TI.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo)) & " eksik"
		Doc.d7MsgBox MsgStr, "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	DagitilacakMiktar = STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if DagitilacakMiktar = 0 Then Exit For
		if i > 1 Then
			if satirNo = STOK20TI.GetRowCount() Then
				satirNo = STOK20TI.AddRow()
			Else
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
				STOK20TI.InsertRowNoUI satirNo
			End if
			STOK20TI.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			TrNum = STOK20TI.TRNUM(satirNo)
			STOK20TI.CopyRowFromOtherTable satirNo, STOK20TI, (satirNo - 1)
			STOK20TI.TRNUM(satirNo) = TrNum
		End if
		if S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) < DagitilacakMiktar Then
			DagitilanMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Else
			DagitilanMiktar = DagitilacakMiktar
		End if

		STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK20TI.SERINO(satirNo) = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK20TI.SF_VRI_VR_C(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK20TI.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK20TI.SF_VRI_VR_R2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK20TI.SF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK20TI.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK20TI.SF_VRI_NUM2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK20TI.FOKOD(satirNo) = S01.FOKOD(i)

		STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = DagitilanMiktar

		DagitilacakMiktar = DagitilacakMiktar - DagitilanMiktar
	Next
End Function
Function SonOpMu(MPSNo, JobNo)
	SonOpMu = False
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", JobNo, "JOBNO")
	if trim(SrNum) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("f23f23sdgh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		SonOpMu = True
	End if
End Function
Function UretimFisiniSil(EvrakNo, TrNum)
	SeriNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SFDC32T", "SERINO", EvrakNo & TrNum, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	QStr = "W_EVRAKTYPE='S4'&W_EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&W_TRNUM='" & TrNum & "'"
	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select1("sdfwegwhs", "STOK20T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S20T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		UretimFisiniSil = 0 
		Exit Function
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher S20T1.EVRAKNO(1)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then 
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Delete_Voucher
		Exit Function
	Else
		For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S20T.SERINO(i)) = trim(SeriNo) Then
				S20T.DeleRowNoUI i
			End if
		Next
		UretimFisiniSil = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	End if
End Function
Sub EtiketleriGuncelle(SFDC32T)
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SFDC32T.SERINO(i)), 30)
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("adf3efsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB.SF_MIKTAR <> SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if Not DocYaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
					Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
					DocYaratildi = True
				End if
				Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
				d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub CalcOpMiktar(MPSNo, IsNo, BuOpTamamlananMiktar, OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sd2g24sdfgdsg", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM<'" & SrNum & "'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = -1
	Else
		BuOpYMamulMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_YMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO")
		OncekiOpTamamlananMiktar = Doc.Div(M10T.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR(1) * BuOpYMamulMiktar, M10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(1))
	End if
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("dfw34gsg", "MAKROTABLET_STACK", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = 0
	if MTableT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar = MTableT.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
	BuOpTamamlananMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO") + BuEvrakTamamlananMiktar + _
						STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
End Sub
Function HammaddeYok(IsNo, UretilecekMiktar)
	HammaddeYok = False
	MPSNo = Left(IsNo, 10)
	SiraNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_SIRANO", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	MPSYMamulMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_YMAMULMIKTAR", IsNo, "JOBNO")

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("df24gsgs", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & SiraNo & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		Depo = Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "IM")
		IhtiyacMiktari = Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(i) *  UretilecekMiktar, MPSYMamulMiktar)
		if Depo < IhtiyacMiktari Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu operasyonda kullanýlan " & trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)) & " kodlu malzeme IM deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeYok = True
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function YariMamulMu(StokKodu)
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("sdfw424dgfg", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & StokKodu & "'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		YariMamulMu = "E"
	Else
		YariMamulMu = "H"
	End if
End Function
Sub Terazi1denOku()
	Call TerazidenOku("10.0.0.135", "TERAZI_1500")
End Sub
Sub Terazi2denOku()
	Call TerazidenOku("10.0.0.134", "TERAZI_100")
End Sub
Sub TerazidenOku(IpAddress, AlanAdi)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	Agirlik = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "GetAgirlikFromTerazi", IpAddress)
	NumuneIP = "10.0.0.133"
	NumuneTerazi = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "GetAgirlikFromTerazi", NumuneIP)

	MTable.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, Agirlik
	MTable.TERAZI_30 = NumuneTerazi
	MTable.BIRIM_AGIRLIK = NumuneTerazi / 10
	MTable.NET_AGIRLIK = Agirlik - MTable.DARA
	
	ToplamMiktar = Doc.Div(MTable.NET_AGIRLIK, NumuneTerazi / 10) + 10 'Numune sayýsý ilave ediliyor.
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = Doc.Round(ToplamMiktar,0)
End Sub
Function GetNextJobNo(IsNo)
	GetNextJobNo = ""
	if trim(IsNo) = "" Then Exit Function

	SRNUM = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	MPSNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	if trim(MPSNo) = "" or trim(SRNUM) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdg24hdfh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SRNUM & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetNextJobNo = M10T.JOBNO(1)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "HMD_KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "HMD_KOD_STOK00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""

	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "BOZUK","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_NO","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SON_OPMU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_SAYISI","DOUBLE",3,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "YARIMAMULMU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SONRAKI_OP","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")

	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSTM_TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""

	MTableT.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"

End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	if trim(MTable.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	Barkod = UCase(Barkod)
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "*", "-")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, ".", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "Ç", "%")
	Barkod = Replace(Barkod, "ç", "%")

	Hata = False
	if Left(Barkod, 4) = "%JB%" Then
		IsNo = Mid(Barkod, 5)
		Call IsBilgileriniDoldur(IsNo)
	Else
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdg34hshrs", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Call StokBilgileriniDoldur(d7LB)
		Else
			Hata = True
		End if
		if Hata Then
 			Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod geçersiz", "", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if

	MTable.BARKOD = ""
End Sub
Sub IsBilgileriniDoldur(IsNo)
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("asdfwrhsfh", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(IsNo), 16) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox IsNo & " no.lu iþ geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub		
	End if

	Set M10T1 = Doc.Select1("__MMPS10T_LIST", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO(1) & "'", "", -1)

	MTable.KOD = M10T.R_MAMULKODU(1)
	MTable.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T.R_MAMULKODU(1), "")
	MTable.MPSNO = M10T.EVRAKNO(1)
	MTable.JOBNO = IsNo
	MTable.MMPS10T_SRNUM = M10T.SRNUM(1)
	MTable.MMPS10T_R_SIRANO = M10T.R_SIRANO(1)
	MTable.OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON(1)
	MTable.OPERASYON_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10T.R_OPERASYON(1), "")
	MTable.TEZGAH = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(1)
	MTable.TEZGAH_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(1), "")
End Sub
Sub StokBilgileriniDoldur(d7LB)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdgqerhsd", "STOK01", "KOD='" & d7LB.SKOD(1) & "'&SERINO='" & d7LB.SERINO(1) & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod stokta bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	STOK01_PDA.HMD_KOD = S01.KOD(1)
	STOK01_PDA.HMD_KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(1), "")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(1)
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(1)
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(1)
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = S01.SERINO(1)

	if OkutulanHammaddeHatali Then STOK01_PDA.Empty
End Sub
Sub cmdOk()
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc2.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set MAKROTABLE_SF = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if trim(STOK01_PDA.SERINO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Hammadde/Yarýmamul Barkodu Okutulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if not HammaddeDepoTransferiOk() Then Exit Sub
	
	Doc2.Load_Voucher STOK01_PDA.SERINO

	d7LB.REFTEXT501 = MAKROTABLE_SF.JOBNO & Doc.BuGun & Doc.Saat(8)
	ret = Doc2.Save_Voucher
	Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		Call EkraniTemizle()
		Doc.DeleteTableObject "__MMPS10T_LIST"
	End if
End Sub
Function HammaddeDepoTransferiOk()
	HammaddeDepoTransferiOk = False
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if trim(STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE) <> "HMD" Then
		HammaddeDepoTransferiOk = True
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdgqerhsd", "STOK01", "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.HMD_KOD & "'&SERINO='" & STOK01_PDA.SERINO & "'&AMBCODE='" & STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod stokta bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK26", "")
	Set STOK26E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set STOK26T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	STOK26E.AMBCODE = "HMD"
	STOK26E.TARGETAMBCODE = "IMD"

	satirNo = STOK26T.AddRow
	STOK26T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	STOK26T.KOD(satirNo) = S01.KOD(1)
	STOK26T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = S01.LOTNUMBER(1)
	STOK26T.SERINO(satirNo) = S01.SERINO(1)
	STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_C(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_C(1)
	STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R1(1)
	STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R2(1)
	STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R3(1)
	STOK26T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM1(1)
	STOK26T.SF_VRI_NUM2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM2(1)
	STOK26T.FOKOD(satirNo) = S01.FOKOD(1)

	STOK26T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)

	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then HammaddeDepoTransferiOk = True
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Function OkutulanHammaddeHatali()
	OkutulanHammaddeHatali = False
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	if STOK01_PDA.HMD_KOD <> MTable.KOD Then
		QStr = "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & MTable.MMPS10T_R_SIRANO & "'&R_KAYNAKKODU='" & STOK01_PDA.HMD_KOD & "'"
		Set M10TH = Doc.Select2("sdfhqejsjd", "__MMPS10T_LIST", QStr, "", -1)

		if M10TH.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu hammadde bu operasyonda kullanýlmamaktadýr", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			OkutulanHammaddeHatali = True
		End if
	Else
		OperasyonSiraNo = GetOperasyonSiraNo(MTable.JOBNO)
		if OperasyonSiraNo = 1 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu operasyonda yarýmamul kullanýlmamaktadýr", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			OkutulanHammaddeHatali = True
		Else
			ToplamOperasyonSayisi = GetToplamOperasyonSayisi()
			LotStr = "-" & OperasyonSiraNo - 1 & "/" & ToplamOperasyonSayisi
			if Len(trim(STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER)) < Len(LotStr) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan etiketin Lot No 'su " & vbcrlf & LotStr & " ile bitmelidir", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
				OkutulanHammaddeHatali = True
			Else
				if Right(trim(STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER), Len(LotStr)) <> LotStr Then 
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan etiketin Lot No 'su " & vbcrlf & LotStr & " ile bitmelidir", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
					OkutulanHammaddeHatali = True
				End if
			End if
		End if
	End if
End Function
Function GetOperasyonSiraNo(IsNo)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	QStr = "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM<'" & MTable.MMPS10T_SRNUM & "'"
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("asdgqeraeadfha", "__MMPS10T_LIST", QStr, "", -1)
	GetOperasyonSiraNo = M10T.GetRowCount + 1
End Function
Function GetToplamOperasyonSayisi()
	Set M10T = Doc.Select2("asdgqeraeadfha", "__MMPS10T_LIST", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	GetToplamOperasyonSayisi = M10T.GetRowCount
End Function
